<h1 class="wp-block-heading">I went on a run.</h1>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">My roommate and I went on a run from out apartment in Westwood around the UCLA campus. He quickly outpaced me about 1/4 of the way through since I hadn&#8217;t run in 7 months, so I took the time to walk back to the apartment through UCLA just to see the place I used call my second home.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">It was empty. This is what I saw.</p>



<p class="image-credit">Photos by Hakan Alpay.</p>



<div style="height:20px;" aria-hidden="true" class="wp-block-spacer"></div>

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">So yeah its a little sad that its gonna be this empty for a while, but on the flip side, I&#8217;ve never gotten a photo of Royce with no people in it, so that&#8217;s nice 🙂</p>
